### 📌 Pilar: Penetration Tester & AppSec (39 hallazgos)

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [CRITICAL] Cryptographic Failure: Insecure Block Cipher Mode (ECB Mode)
- **ID:** `CRYPTO-CIPHER-ECB-MODE-01` | **Herramienta:** `Cryptographic Failures Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\modules\penetration_tester\crypto_failures_scanner.py` (Línea 40)
- **CWE:** `CWE-327`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1027` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
ECB (Electronic Codebook) mode encrypts identical plaintext blocks into identical ciphertext blocks, revealing data patterns and vulnerable to block replay attacks.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
r'(?:AES\.MODE_ECB|modes\.ECB\(\)|AES/ECB/PKCS5Padding|Cipher\.getInstance\s*\(\s*["\']AES["\'])',
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Use authenticated encryption modes like AES-GCM (Galois/Counter Mode) or ChaCha20-Poly1305 with unique nonces.

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [CRITICAL] Insecure Deserialization: Untrusted Object Deserialization (pickle / unserialize)
- **ID:** `APPSEC-DESERIAL-PICKLE-01` | **Herramienta:** `Command Injection & Deserialization Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\modules\penetration_tester\deserialization_scanner.py` (Línea 4)
- **CWE:** `CWE-502`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1190` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
Deserializing untrusted data streams (via Python pickle, PHP unserialize, or PyYAML unsafe loader) allows arbitrary code execution via magic methods (__reduce__, __wakeup__).

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
- Python: pickle.loads(), _pickle.loads(), marshal.loads(), shelve.open() (CWE-502)
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Use safe serialization formats like JSON, Protocol Buffers, or `yaml.safe_load()`. Never deserialize raw binary streams from untrusted sources.

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [CRITICAL] Insecure Deserialization: Python pickle / marshal / shelve Ingestion
- **ID:** `APPSEC-DESERIAL-PYTHON-PICKLE-01` | **Herramienta:** `Insecure Deserialization Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\modules\penetration_tester\deserialization_scanner.py` (Línea 4)
- **CWE:** `CWE-502`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1190` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
Deserializing data with Python's standard `pickle` or `marshal` module executes arbitrary code embedded in the serialized stream via the `__reduce__` or `__reduce_ex__` magic methods. Pickle is explicitly not secure against erroneous or maliciously constructed data.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
pickle.loads(
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Replace pickle with safe, standard data interchange formats such as JSON, Protocol Buffers, or MessagePack. If binary serialization is mandatory, sign payloads with HMAC (SHA-256) and verify the signature before unpickling.

---

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [CRITICAL] Insecure Deserialization: PyYAML yaml.load() Without SafeLoader
- **ID:** `APPSEC-DESERIAL-PYYAML-UNSAFE-01` | **Herramienta:** `Insecure Deserialization Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\modules\penetration_tester\deserialization_scanner.py` (Línea 5)
- **CWE:** `CWE-502`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1190` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
Calling `yaml.load()` without specifying `Loader=yaml.SafeLoader` permits execution of arbitrary Python code through YAML tags such as `!!python/object/apply:os.system`.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
yaml.load()
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Always use `yaml.safe_load(stream)` or explicitly provide `Loader=yaml.SafeLoader`.

---

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [CRITICAL] Insecure Deserialization: Node.js node-serialize.unserialize() Execution
- **ID:** `APPSEC-DESERIAL-NODE-SERIALIZE-01` | **Herramienta:** `Insecure Deserialization Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\modules\penetration_tester\deserialization_scanner.py` (Línea 6)
- **CWE:** `CWE-502`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1190` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
The `node-serialize` package executes Immediately Invoked Function Expressions (IIFE) embedded in serialized payloads (`_$$ND_FUNC$$_...()`), leading to remote code execution in Node.js.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
serialize.unserialize(
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Remove `node-serialize`. Parse data using standard `JSON.parse()` with schema validation (e.g. Zod or AJV).

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [CRITICAL] Insecure Deserialization: Java ObjectInputStream.readObject() / XMLDecoder
- **ID:** `APPSEC-DESERIAL-JAVA-OBJECTINPUT-01` | **Herramienta:** `Insecure Deserialization Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\modules\penetration_tester\deserialization_scanner.py` (Línea 7)
- **CWE:** `CWE-502`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1190` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
Java `readObject()` deserializes arbitrary byte streams. When gadget classes (e.g. Apache Commons Collections, Spring, Groovy) exist on the classpath, an attacker can construct chains leading to arbitrary command execution.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
ObjectInputStream.readObject(
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Migrate from Java native serialization to JSON/protobuf. If legacy serialization is required, implement an `ObjectInputFilter` (JEP 290) to strictly whitelist authorized classes.

---

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [CRITICAL] Insecure Deserialization: .NET BinaryFormatter.Deserialize()
- **ID:** `APPSEC-DESERIAL-DOTNET-BINARYFORMATTER-01` | **Herramienta:** `Insecure Deserialization Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\modules\penetration_tester\deserialization_scanner.py` (Línea 8)
- **CWE:** `CWE-502`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1190` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
`BinaryFormatter` in .NET is inherently insecure and cannot be made secure. Deserializing untrusted binary streams allows attackers to instantiate arbitrary types and achieve code execution via type confusion gadget chains.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
BinaryFormatter.Deserialize(
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Microsoft officially discourages `BinaryFormatter`. Replace with `System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer` or `XmlSerializer`.

`BinaryFormatter` in .NET is inherently insecure and cannot be made secure. Deserializing untrusted binary streams allows attackers to instantiate arbitrary types and achieve code execution via type confusion gadget chains.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
BinaryFormatter.Deserialize(
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Microsoft officially discourages `BinaryFormatter`. Replace with `System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer` or `XmlSerializer`.

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [CRITICAL] Command Injection: Subprocess Invoked with shell=True
- **ID:** `APPSEC-CMDI-SUBPROCESS-01` | **Herramienta:** `Command Injection & Deserialization Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\qa\dynamic_pocs.py` (Línea 120)
- **CWE:** `CWE-78`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1059` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
Executing system commands through a shell interpreter (`shell=True`) with dynamic variables permits arbitrary operating system command execution (RCE).

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
proc = subprocess.run(cmd_unsafe, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Set `shell=False` and pass arguments as an explicit array of strings: `subprocess.run(['ping', '-c', '1', host])`.

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [CRITICAL] Insecure Deserialization: Untrusted Object Deserialization (pickle / unserialize)
- **ID:** `APPSEC-DESERIAL-PICKLE-01` | **Herramienta:** `Command Injection & Deserialization Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\qa\dynamic_pocs.py` (Línea 300)
- **CWE:** `CWE-502`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1190` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
Deserializing untrusted data streams (via Python pickle, PHP unserialize, or PyYAML unsafe loader) allows arbitrary code execution via magic methods (__reduce__, __wakeup__).

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
untrusted_object = pickle.loads(malicious_bytes)
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Use safe serialization formats like JSON, Protocol Buffers, or `yaml.safe_load()`. Never deserialize raw binary streams from untrusted sources.

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [CRITICAL] Insecure Deserialization: Python pickle / marshal / shelve Ingestion
- **ID:** `APPSEC-DESERIAL-PYTHON-PICKLE-01` | **Herramienta:** `Insecure Deserialization Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\qa\dynamic_pocs.py` (Línea 300)
- **CWE:** `CWE-502`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1190` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
Deserializing data with Python's standard `pickle` or `marshal` module executes arbitrary code embedded in the serialized stream via the `__reduce__` or `__reduce_ex__` magic methods. Pickle is explicitly not secure against erroneous or maliciously constructed data.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
pickle.loads(
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Replace pickle with safe, standard data interchange formats such as JSON, Protocol Buffers, or MessagePack. If binary serialization is mandatory, sign payloads with HMAC (SHA-256) and verify the signature before unpickling.

#### 🟣 [POR DISEÑO] [HIGH] Server-Side Request Forgery: Dynamic URL in Outbound HTTP Request
- **ID:** `APPSEC-SSRF-OUTBOUND-01` | **Herramienta:** `SSRF, Path Traversal & XXE Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\ai\co_analyst.py` (Línea 158)
- **CWE:** `CWE-918`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1190` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
Outbound HTTP request executed against a dynamic, user-supplied URL without private subnet or cloud metadata (169.254.169.254) validation.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as response:
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Validate target URLs against an explicit allowlist. Disallow private IP ranges (RFC 1918) and cloud instance metadata addresses.

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [LOW] Cryptographic Agility: Classical Asymmetric Cryptography Without PQC Hybrid Shielding
- **ID:** `CRYPTO-PQC-AGILITY-ADVISORY` | **Herramienta:** `Cryptographic Agility & Post-Quantum (PQC) Readiness Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\modules\penetration_tester\crypto_pqc_agility_scanner.py` (Línea 138)
- **CWE:** `CWE-327`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1553` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
The module utilizes classical public-key cryptography (RSA/ECDH/ECDSA). Under Shor's Algorithm running on Cryptanalytically Relevant Quantum Computers (CRQCs), all standard discrete-log and factorization schemes will be compromised. Sensitive data with high longevity is vulnerable to 'Harvest Now, Decrypt Later' (HNDL).

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
Classical Asymmetric PKI / Key Exchange in use
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Incorporate Cryptographic Agility architecture: isolate cryptographic primitives behind abstraction interfaces to enable plug-and-play migration to NIST FIPS 203 (ML-KEM / Kyber) and NIST FIPS 204 (ML-DSA / Dilithium) hybrid key exchange schemes.

- **ID:** `CLEAN-HARDCODED-LOCAL-ENDPOINT` | **Herramienta:** `Clean Code, Anti-Hardcoding & Best Practices Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\qa\user_simulation.py` (Línea 477)
- **CWE:** `CWE-588`

**Descripción:**
The application contains a hardcoded local URL/IP endpoint ('"http://127.0.0.1:8000"'). If deployed to staging or production, network requests will target the wrong host or fail completely.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
def run_user_simulation_cli(base_url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:8000", headless: bool = True) -> UserSimulationReport:
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Externalize API base URLs into environment variables (e.g. process.env.API_BASE_URL or os.environ.get('API_URL')).

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [LOW] Deeply Nested Ternary Operator (Cognitive Complexity Anti-Pattern)
- **ID:** `CLEAN-NESTED-TERNARY` | **Herramienta:** `Clean Code, Anti-Hardcoding & Best Practices Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\core\remediation.py` (Línea 275)
- **CWE:** `CWE-1078`

**Descripción:**
The code contains multiple nested ternary operators ('? :') on the same statement. Nested ternaries severely impair readability, impede code review comprehension, and are a notorious source of off-by-one logic errors.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
r"(?:^|\n)###\s*(?:\[(SUBSANADO|RESOLVED|POR DISEÑO|AS_DESIGNED|OPEN|PENDIENTE)\]\s*)?`?([A-Za-z0-9_\-\.:]+)`?(?::\s*([^\n]+))?\n(.*?)(?=\n###|\n## |\Z)",
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Refactor nested ternaries into clear 'if-else' statements, guard clauses, or a lookup dictionary/map.

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [LOW] Software QA: Time-of-Check to Time-of-Use (TOCTOU) Race Condition
- **ID:** `QA-RACE-TOCTOU-FILE` | **Herramienta:** `Concurrency & Race Condition Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\core\reverifier.py` (Línea 286)
- **CWE:** `CWE-362`

**Descripción:**
File existence verified prior to opening/deleting rather than handling the operation atomically. Between check and use, an external process can modify, delete, or substitute the target path (TOCTOU).

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
if os.path.exists(overrides_file):
                    with open(
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Perform the file operation directly inside a `try/except (FileNotFoundError, FileExistsError)` block or use atomic flags (`os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL`).

```
            with open(
```

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [LOW] Deeply Nested Ternary Operator (Cognitive Complexity Anti-Pattern)
- **ID:** `CLEAN-NESTED-TERNARY` | **Herramienta:** `Clean Code, Anti-Hardcoding & Best Practices Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\web\templates\index.html` (Línea 1855)
- **CWE:** `CWE-1078`

**Descripción:**
The code contains multiple nested ternary operators ('? :') on the same statement. Nested ternaries severely impair readability, impede code review comprehension, and are a notorious source of off-by-one logic errors.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
const url = effectiveProjectId ? `/api/findings/reverify-all?project_id=${encodeURIComponent(effectiveProjectId)}` : '/api/findings/reverify-all';
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Refactor nested ternaries into clear 'if-else' statements, guard clauses, or a lookup dictionary/map.

- **ID:** `AISEC-SUPPLY-CHAIN-PICKLE-WEIGHTS-01` | **Herramienta:** `AI Supply Chain & Model Checkpoint Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\modules\ai_security\ai_supply_chain_scanner.py` (Línea 34)
- **CWE:** `CWE-502`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1195.002` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
Loading machine learning model checkpoints using Python pickle or `torch.load(..., weights_only=False)` permits arbitrary code execution (RCE) embedded inside crafted weights (__reduce__ payload).

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
"description": "Loading machine learning model checkpoints using Python pickle or `torch.load(..., weights_only=False)` permits arbitrary code execution (RCE) embedded inside craft
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Migrate to `safetensors` format (`safetensors.torch.load_file()`) or enforce `torch.load(..., weights_only=True)` on PyTorch 2.4+.

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [HIGH] Network Security: Insecure Plaintext Protocol in Use (Telnet / FTP / Plain LDAP)
- **ID:** `HARDENING-CLEARTEXT-PROTOCOLS-01` | **Herramienta:** `System & Network Hardening Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\modules\security_analyst\lolbas_detector.py` (Línea 51)
- **CWE:** `CWE-319`
- **MITRE ATT&CK:** `T1040` — *Técnica General* (Táctica)

**Descripción:**
Application or configuration uses legacy plaintext network protocols (Telnet, FTP, plain LDAP, or default SNMP community strings), transmitting credentials in cleartext.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
r"regsvr32(?:\.exe)?\s+(?:/[a-zA-Z]+\s+)*(?:/i:https?://|/i:ftp://).*scrobj\.dll",
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Replace plaintext protocols with encrypted equivalents: SSH/SFTP instead of Telnet/FTP, LDAPS (port 636) instead of plain LDAP, and SNMPv3 with authPriv.

### 📌 Pilar: UI/UX & Accessibility (a11y) (39 hallazgos)

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [MEDIUM] UI/UX a11y: Icon-Only Button Without Accessible Label (WCAG 4.1.2)
- **ID:** `UIUX-A11Y-UNLABELLED-BUTTON` | **Herramienta:** `UI/UX Accessibility & WCAG Standards Scanner`
- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\web\templates\index.html` (Línea 1682)
- **CWE:** `CWE-1078`

**Descripción:**
Interactive button or anchor contains only an icon with no text or `aria-label`. Assistive technologies will announce 'button' with no name or purpose.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
<a href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/${f.cwe_id.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')}.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="text-xs text-amber-400 hover:text-amber-30
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Add an explicit `aria-label="Description of action"` or a visually hidden text element (`<span class="sr-only">...</span>`).

#### 🔴 [PENDIENTE] [LOW] UI/UX Typography: Sub-Readable Font Size Below 11px

- **Ubicación:** `c:\Users\psypp\OneDrive\Escritorio\W\cyberaudit\web\templates\index.html` (Línea 113)
- **CWE:** `CWE-1078`

**Descripción:**
Font size declared at 10px or smaller. Extreme small type severely degrades legibility, causes eye fatigue, and renders information unreadable on high-DPI displays.

**Fragmento de Código Detectado:**
```
text-[10px]
```

**Remediación Sugerida:**
Maintain a minimum font size of 12px for microcopy/labels and 14-16px for body copy.
